# Skills · C · part 5

881 entries, cadrage to calc-demo. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **cadrage**: Le comportement du cadrage automatique d'avolo-shorts — le choix du ratio, du crop, du **split-screen** et de la **composition du doublage improvisé**, ce qui a déjà été essayé et écarté, les pièges qui ne se voient pas, et comment mesurer un changement. À lire avant de toucher à `src/core/framing.ts`, `src/core/dubbing.ts`, `worker/detect.py`, `src/core/ffmpeg/args.ts`, `scripts/framing/**` ou `analysis.json`, et avant de régler quoi que ce soit dans `FRAMING_DEFAULTS`. À lire aussi quand quelqu'un dit qu'un clip sort « trop large », qu'un ratio est « mauvais », que le cadre « coupe quelqu'u… ([JulienCr/avolo-shorts](https://github.com/JulienCr/avolo-shorts/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cadrage))
- **cadrage**: Établit avec l'utilisateur le cahier des charges d'un site (CADRAGE.md) — objectif, catalogue, fonctionnalités du lancement et critères d'acceptation, paiement, livraison, obligations légales, questions ouvertes — puis le contrôle. À utiliser avant de coder les fonctionnalités d'un site, quand un client exprime ses besoins, ou quand le périmètre d'un site change. ([Lbatisseur/projet-claude](https://github.com/Lbatisseur/projet-claude/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cadrage))
- **cadrage**: Cadrer une idée avant tout travail. Produit cadrage.md et la chaîne de cartes dans `docs/cartes/`. À utiliser quand le fondateur donne une idée nouvelle. ([Lugdunoob/regie-claude](https://github.com/Lugdunoob/regie-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/cadrage))
- **cadrage**: Ouvre un nouveau chantier — rédige son plan dans docs/chantiers/plan-&lt;nom&gt;.md, découpé en lots livrables, le fait relire par les experts utiles et le soumet à la validation de Quentin avant toute ligne de code. À utiliser quand Quentin lance une nouvelle étape d'envergure du pipeline. ([Quentronpers/SecuExtractor](https://github.com/Quentronpers/SecuExtractor/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cadrage))
- **cadrage**: Flow de cadrage interview pour structurer un projet ou une note du carnet. ([syskin/mathieu-cadu](https://github.com/syskin/mathieu-cadu/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cadrage))
- **cadrage-acces**: Cadrage d'une intervention SonarQube et accès à l'instance (choix des missions et du périmètre, modes de connexion au choix, détection de la version, de l'édition et du mode, jeton du compte Sonar en variable d'environnement, appels de lecture libres, appels d'écriture validés, catalogue des appels d'API, analyse sous une branche de travail et outil MCP admis pour l'examen local). - **cadrage-audit**: Audite un existant de projet - cahier des charges, expression de besoin, projet déjà engagé, solution en place, dossier d'appel d'offres - pour en dégager ce qui est établi, les contradictions, les lacunes, les hypothèses implicites et les risques, avant de poursuivre. À utiliser quand l'utilisateur fournit un document ou décrit un projet en cours et demande une relecture critique, un audit, un diagnostic de l'existant ou un avis avant de continuer (requirements review, project audit, tender review). Fait partie de la famille cadrage-projet. ([Elmane091203/cadrage-projet](https://github.com/Elmane091203/cadrage-projet/tree/HEAD/plugins/cadrage-projet/skills/cadrage-audit))
- **cadrage-canon**: Rédige un cadrage dans le référentiel SSK Canon du projet courant — l'unité de changement datée qui décrit ce qu'on veut faire, pourquoi, et quelles règles de gestion en découlent —, puis propose d'ouvrir le chantier sur les dépôts de code : une issue portant le besoin par dépôt impacté, et le lien retour dans le cadrage. Le référentiel vit dans un dépôt à part, que ce skill trouve seul. À utiliser quand l'utilisateur dit "cadre cette évolution", "rédige le cadrage", "consigne cette décision", ou avant d'ouvrir un chantier dont les règles métier ne sont pas encore posées. ([ssk-it/ssk-canon-action](https://github.com/ssk-it/ssk-canon-action/tree/HEAD/skills/cadrage-canon))
- **cadrage-canon-update**: Met à jour le skill cadrage-canon — le corriger, l'enrichir, ou le remettre à niveau depuis sa source publiée. À utiliser quand l'utilisateur signale un défaut du skill de cadrage, veut y ajouter quelque chose, ou demande de le mettre à jour. Le skill vit dans ssk-it/ssk-canon-action et non chez le développeur : ce skill-ci dit par où passer pour que les exemplaires ne divergent pas. - **cadrage-cles-de-voute**: Cadrer une séquence du dépôt en-ligne-pour-le-cycle-4 avant de l'écrire, puis l'auditer avant de la valider, avec les sept clés de voûte (n°306, 87/311, 337, 343, 346, 347, 348), une checklist de dix-huit questions à réponse vérifiable et une grille sur 100 points où un zéro aux critères 1 ou 2 bloque. À utiliser au début de tout lot, à chaque révision, et pour noter une page après un audit externe. - **cady**: Reads the company's context in Cady, then loads the matching skill. Use when the work is a company's sales and outreach, drafting to a person (read the sender's voice pack first), posts, competitors and pricing, tools and integrations, budget, fundraising, filings and hiring, product and engineering work, and company agents. Not for personal tasks or coding with no company context. Requires a Cady account. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/cady/skills/cady))
- **cady-accounting-method**: Accounting role card: what this function owns and decides. Load when acting as the Accounting agent or choosing among its skills. Not for doing a task: load the task skill. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-accounting-method))
- **cady-accounting-period-close**: Month-end and period close. Use when: Close the books for a month, quarter or year: reconcile bank, payouts and revenue, clear Suspense and lock. Not for: Preparing a tax return (use `cady-finance-tax-cycle`), budgets or runway, or audit sign-off. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-accounting-period-close))
- **cady-company-change-agent-behaviour**: Change agent behaviour. Use when: Change what agents do: edit a skill, role card, run sequence, tool text or an agent's goal. Not for: Making, scheduling or retiring an agent (`cady-company-manage-agents`), or writing or judging a post or design. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-change-agent-behaviour))
- **cady-company-decision**: Who decides, and how to record it. Use when: Before a choice or an effect: whether it is decided, who decides, how to test a big call and how to record it. Not for: Gathering the evidence a call needs (`cady-company-evidence`), the weekly goal calls (`cady-company-weekly-review`), or doing the task itself. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-decision))
- **cady-company-evidence**: Key questions and behaviour tests. Use when: A decision you own needs evidence: frame its questions, take the cheapest route, pre-register any test of behaviour. Not for: Dashboards, reports or data no decision needs; company goals and strategy (`cady-company-weekly-review`); deciding the answer for another owner; routine delivery whose desired behaviour is clear (`cady-engineering-method`). ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-evidence))
- **cady-company-manage-agents**: Manage company agents. Use when: Create and bind, schedule, review or retire a company agent: whether one is warranted, its design and first task. Not for: Native helpers, subagents, one-off work items and agents on a host that is in contact. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-manage-agents))
- **cady-company-method**: Executive role card: its point and key bets. Load when acting as the Executive agent (the outer loop). Not for doing a task: load the task skill. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-method))
- **cady-company-onboarding**: Set up a company and hand over to its Executive. Use when: A new company has no Cady setup yet: record it from what the owner says and set up its Executive agent. Not for: Designing or taking an agent live (`cady-company-manage-agents`), or building a first offering (`cady-gtm-launch`). ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-onboarding))
- **cady-company-operations-compliance-register**: Compliance register and monthly filing review. Use when: What statutory and registry filings the company owes and when (annual returns, confirmation statements, state reports). Not for: Preparing one tax return (`cady-finance-tax-cycle`), submitting any filing or payment, or legal advice. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-operations-compliance-register))
- **cady-company-operations-contract-checks**: Contract checks: before signing, after completion. Use when: A contract that commits the company is about to be signed or was just signed: what to settle first and what it starts. Not for: Posts or updates about it, the yearly filing calendar (`cady-company-operations-compliance-register`), general legal questions, or a contract not yet in view. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-operations-contract-checks))
- **cady-company-operations-inbox-follow-through**: Inbox follow-through: group, triage, track commitments. Use when: Bounded inbox maintenance or thread triage for follow-through, marketing handling or commitments needing tracking. Not for: Executing a prospect reply (`cady-gtm-reply`), sending or drafting outreach, or reading mail Cady is not given: only the host's connector reads mail. Thread triage remains in scope. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-operations-inbox-follow-through))
- **cady-company-operations-method**: Company Operations role card: what this function owns and decides. Load when acting as the Company Operations agent or choosing among its skills. Not for doing a task: load the task skill. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-operations-method))
- **cady-company-operations-people-lifecycle**: Joiners, movers and leavers. Use when: Plan, track and verify the paperwork and access for a new hire or contractor, a role change or someone leaving. Not for: Running payroll, buying licences, or employment or immigration legal advice. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-operations-people-lifecycle))
- **cady-company-operations-requirement-register**: Requirement register: research, check and record. Use when: Research, independently check and record one compliance requirement as a Cady Projects decision. Not for: Tracking occurrences or events (the calling skill does that), filing, signing or legal advice. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-operations-requirement-register))
- **cady-company-taste-review**: Writing craft and taste decisions. Use when: Write or edit a post, article, web copy or other public text, or review a design (judge with the taste check). Not for: Pass/fail checks a script or test can decide (run those first), a direct message to one person, or writing a prompt or skill and changing how agents behave (`cady-company-change-agent-behaviour`). ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-taste-review))
- **cady-company-weekly-review**: Weekly review. Use when: The weekly review: assess the company, call every goal and initiative, check bets, hand changes to the orchestrator. Not for: A new company's setup (`cady-company-onboarding`), pushing work along, scheduling or dispatching agents or doing function work. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-weekly-review))
- **cady-customer-success-lifecycle**: Customer lifecycle: first value to renewal. Use when: Run one customer account after the sale: onboarding to first value, health, risk, renewal, expansion or cancellation. Not for: Open deals before signature (`cady-sales-deal-motion`), the company's own vendor renewals (`cady-finance-vendor-spend-review`), or message copy alone (`cady-gtm-reply` or `cady-gtm-warm-email`). ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-customer-success-lifecycle))
- **cady-customer-success-method**: Customer Success role card: what this function owns and decides. Load when acting as the Customer Success agent or choosing among its skills. Not for doing a task: load the task skill. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-customer-success-method))
- **cady-design-method**: Design role card: what this function owns and decides. Load when acting as the Design agent or choosing among its skills. Not for doing a task: load the task skill. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-design-method))
- **cady-design-wcag-audit**: Accessibility audit (WCAG 2.2 AA). Use when: Audit a web or app interface against WCAG 2.2 AA with real tools and report ranked, reproducible findings. Not for: Visual direction or layout critique (use `cady-design-method`), functional journey QA (use `cady-qa-method`) or a legal conformance statement (needs a qualified specialist). ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-design-wcag-audit))
- **cady-engineering-debugging**: Debugging and incident response. Use when: Find and fix the root cause of a bug, failing check or production incident, then the regression test and postmortem. Not for: Rating a bug's severity or triaging a flaky test, reviewing someone's diff (use `cady-engineering-independent-review`) or recurring backlog review (use `cady-engineering-system-cycle`). ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-engineering-debugging))
- **cady-engineering-independent-review**: Independent review. Independently review a diff, artifact or decision record: rerun its checks, rank findings and return land, fix or redesign. Use when: a change needs review, before merge or in a later batch, or a decision others will build on before it is acted on. Not for: root-causing a bug, triaging a bug report or running helpers (`cady-orchestrator-goal-pass`). ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-engineering-independent-review))
- **cady-engineering-integrations**: Integrations. Choose, operate or build a company integration: compare and pick a tool or vendor to connect (the vendor reference), operate a chosen connector (the connector reference) or build one (the build reference). Use when: comparing, choosing or connecting a tool, data source or account. Not for: a purchase or renewal decision on cost (use `cady-finance-vendor-spend-review`). ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-engineering-integrations))
- **cady-engineering-method**: Engineering role card: what this function owns and decides. Load when acting as the Engineering agent or choosing among its skills. Not for doing a task: load the task skill. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-engineering-method))
- **cady-engineering-system-cycle**: Recurring system cycle: map, dimensions, routed motions. Use when: Clear a product's engineering backlog in recurring cycles from its goal map, system dimensions and state-routed motions. Not for: One bounded change with clear acceptance (use `cady-engineering-method`) or deciding what to build and for whom (use `cady-product-manage`). ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-engineering-system-cycle))
- **cady-finance-budget-review**: Budget and cost review. Use when: Draft or update a budget or spend allocation, or review last month's spend against it. Not for: Burn, runway and when to cut, posting ledger entries, reserving money or authorizing payment; use `cady-accounting-method` for close work. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-finance-budget-review))
- **cady-finance-method**: Finance role card: what this function owns and decides. Load when acting as the Finance agent or choosing among its skills. Not for doing a task: load the task skill. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-finance-method))
- **cady-finance-runway**: Burn and runway. Use when: Work out burn, runway months and default alive or dead from actual cash, then decide when to cut and what first. Not for: Drafting a budget or allocation (use `cady-finance-budget-review`), deciding a raise (use `cady-fundraise-raise-plan`), approving spend or moving money. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-finance-runway))
- **cady-finance-tax-cycle**: Entity tax and filing cycle. Use when: Plan or review an entity's tax obligations, filing preparation and material strategy changes. Not for: Filing, payment, legal advice, invented jurisdiction rules or a universal daily tax agent; use `cady-accounting-method` for period close. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-finance-tax-cycle))
- **cady-finance-vendor-spend-review**: Vendor spend review: new purchase or renewal. Use when: Decide whether to buy, trial, renew or cancel a paid tool or vendor: cost, actual use, alternatives and commitment. Not for: Bookkeeping, a finance ledger, payment, provider billing changes, supplier order execution or technical qualification; use `cady-finance-budget-review` for the current budget plan. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-finance-vendor-spend-review))
- **cady-fundraise-investor-list**: Ranked investor list. Use when: Decide who to pitch for a round: a ranked list of named investors and partners, sourced, with warm paths. Not for: Deciding whether, when or how much to raise (use `cady-fundraise-raise-plan`), drafting materials, setting terms or contacting investors. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-fundraise-investor-list))
- **cady-fundraise-investor-outreach**: Investor outreach and updates. Use when: Write to investors: a warm intro request, a personalised first note, round follow-ups, or the monthly update send. Not for: Choosing which investors to pitch (use `cady-fundraise-investor-list`), deciding the round (use `cady-fundraise-raise-plan`) or drafting the deck, memo or update (use `cady-fundraise-raise-materials`). ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-fundraise-investor-outreach))
- **cady-fundraise-method**: Fundraise role card: what this function owns and decides. Load when acting as the Fundraise agent or choosing among its skills. Not for doing a task: load the task skill. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-fundraise-method))
- **cady-fundraise-raise-materials**: Raise materials, data room and investor updates. Use when: Draft or revise an investor memo, deck narrative, data room or monthly investor update for a founder. Not for: Deciding amount, instrument or terms (use `cady-fundraise-raise-plan`), choosing which investors to pitch, sending, or editing the cap table. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-fundraise-raise-materials))
- **cady-fundraise-raise-plan**: Raise plan: amount, instrument, dilution and process. Use when: Decide whether, when and how much to raise (SAFE, note or priced round), model dilution and run the round. Not for: Building the investor list, drafting the deck, memo or update, writing to investors, or legal and tax advice. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-fundraise-raise-plan))
- **cady-gtm-calls**: Sales calls: prepare and follow up. Use when: Prepare for a sales or customer call (account brief, agenda, questions), then record its follow-up and next step. Not for: Dialling, recording audio, cold email, or an unverified call note; use `cady-gtm-warm-email` for a separately authorized message. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-gtm-calls))
- **cady-gtm-channel-plan**: Channel plan. Use when: Turn accepted persona, offer and chosen channels into channel plays and execution briefs. Not for: Choosing the persona, offer or channel mix; researching parties, writing copy, sending or installing schedules. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-gtm-channel-plan))
- **cady-gtm-cold-email-cycle**: Cold email cycle. Use when: Write and send cold email to people not yet contacted, using one template per segment. Not for: Engaged parties, partner or organiser business approaches, replies in a live thread, and every follow-up; use `cady-gtm-warm-email` for partner approaches and follow-ups, or `cady-gtm-reply` for inbound replies. Missing verification holds release, not preparation. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-gtm-cold-email-cycle))
- **cady-gtm-events**: Events: attend or host. Use when: Prepare for or host the event named in the brief to advance the company through real conversations. Not for: Choosing an event or treating an attendee directory as intent. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-gtm-events))
- **cady-gtm-launch**: GTM launch session. Prepare a coordinated go-to-market launch from strategy through approved work. Use when: the company is starting a growth motion. Not for: choosing a persona (use `cady-gtm-offer-and-segments`) or drafting outreach. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-gtm-launch))
- **cady-gtm-linkedin-message**: Social direct message. Use when: Draft a first social DM, LinkedIn connection note or one no-reply follow-up. Not for: Public comments or posts are publishing; email or a reply in a live thread use their own skills. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-gtm-linkedin-message))
- **cady-gtm-method**: GTM role card: what this function owns and decides. Load when acting as the GTM agent or choosing among its skills. Not for doing a task: load the task skill. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-gtm-method))
- **cady-gtm-offer-and-segments**: Offer and persona. Use when: Choose or revise the target persona, offer or message angle from evidence, before a cold cycle and at its review. Not for: Preparing or releasing a message; use `cady-gtm-cold-email-cycle`, `cady-gtm-warm-email`, `cady-gtm-reply` or `cady-gtm-linkedin-message`. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-gtm-offer-and-segments))
- **cady-gtm-persona-profile**: Persona profile. Use when: Write or revise one persona's profile (customer, investor, partner or supplier) before positioning, sourcing or copy. Not for: Choosing the offer or promise (`cady-gtm-offer-and-segments`), sourcing a List (`cady-gtm-tam-map`) or preparing a message. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-gtm-persona-profile))
- **cady-gtm-pipeline-pass**: Warm pipeline pass. Use when: Pass over due tracks of one motion (customer for GTM, investor for fundraising, partner) and their next steps. Not for: Writing one message (use `cady-gtm-warm-email`, `cady-gtm-reply` or `cady-gtm-linkedin-message`) or working one deal in depth. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-gtm-pipeline-pass))
- **cady-gtm-reply**: Reply in a live thread. Use when: Answer an inbound reply in a live thread: a question, interest, objection, referral, not now or stop. Not for: First touches and follow-ups to someone who has not replied. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-gtm-reply))
- **cady-gtm-tam-map**: TAM map. Use when: Find who wants, where they gather: people, communities, prospects, leads, target customers, companies or accounts. Not for: Trigger-only lists or preparing messages; use `cady-gtm-cold-email-cycle` or `cady-gtm-linkedin-message` for outreach. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-gtm-tam-map))
- **cady-gtm-voice-pack**: Build or refresh a texture-only voice pack. Use when: Owner sets up or refreshes how their messages sound, or drafts read generic. Builds the pack from their sent messages. Not for: Drafting rules (relationship, substance, ask: `references/outreach-drafting.md`, sender_voice), reviewing or sending outreach (`cady-gtm-cold-email-cycle`, `cady-gtm-warm-email`), company brand voice (`cady-company-taste-review`). ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-gtm-voice-pack))
- **cady-gtm-warm-email**: Warm email and follow-ups. Use when: Write email follow-ups, engaged notes, or partner, organiser and supplier business messages, including first approaches. Not for: A customer cold first touch, a reply in a live thread, or LinkedIn. Missing history is not proof that a party is cold. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-gtm-warm-email))
- **cady-marketing-method**: Marketing role card: what this function owns and decides. Load when acting as the Marketing agent or choosing among its skills. Not for doing a task: load the task skill. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-marketing-method))
- **cady-marketing-post**: Post one piece. Use when: Create or edit one post for the audience, topic and placement chosen in the brief. Not for: Choosing a content strategy, pillars or distribution plan. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-marketing-post))
- **cady-orchestrator-challenge**: Challenge a result by its stakes. Use when: Reading a result one level down before accepting it, as the orchestrator. - **cafe24-ca-api-export**: Cafe24 Analytics(CA) API 베타로 상품별 노출수(hits)/담김수(cart action)/판매건수(sales) 데이터를 적재하고 전환율(sales/hits)을 계산한다. 이그니스 인프라개발팀 슬랙 요청 "상품 상세 페이지 개선 전후 전환율 측정"에 직접 대응. egnis-mcp에서 cafe24_get_access_token으로 토큰을 발급받아 Python이 ca-api.cafe24data.com을 직접 호출하여 CSV 3종(product_hits.csv, cart_actions.csv, product_sales.csv)과 전환율 매트릭스 HTML 대시보드(reports/cafe24/&lt;brand&gt;/&lt;period&gt;/dashboard.html)를 저장한다. "/cafe24-ca-api-export", "전환율 측정", "CA API 적재"를 호출하거나 --brand --period 옵션을 줄 때 동작한다. ([egnis-it/claude-plugins](https://github.com/egnis-it/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/egnis-cafe24/skills/cafe24-ca-api-export))

Traces each mall's live page to its Cafe24 source (module/variable, admin editor field, skin JS, third-party app) and records the findings as a category in the 자사몰-구현-현황 doc. - **cafe24-doc-update**: 이번 대화 세션에서 새로 알게 된 Cafe24/SmartDesign 플랫폼의 특성·동작을 추려서, cafe24 공용 CLAUDE.md(또는 몰 특정이면 해당 프로젝트 CLAUDE.md)에 그 문서의 기존 톤·구조에 맞춰 반영한다. 세션에서 실측으로 확인한 비자명한 카페24 동작(옵티마이저 번들 거동, UA 스킨 분기, 템플릿/모듈 특성 등)을 다음 세션이 재사용하도록 문서화하는 게 목적. Use this whenever the user wants to record/persist Cafe24 platform insights discovered during work — triggers like "이 세션에서 알게 된 카페24 특성 정리해서 CLAUDE.md에 넣어줘", "방금 알아낸 거 카페24 문서에 업데이트", "카페24 인사이트 반영해줘", "/cafe24-doc-update", "add what we learned about Cafe24 to the notes". 세션 끝/인사이트 발견 직후에 제안해도 좋다. ([sadie100/claude-dotfiles](https://github.com/sadie100/claude-dotfiles/tree/HEAD/skills/cafe24-doc-update))

- **cafe24-find-source**: 라이브 Cafe24 페이지에서 렌더된 스타일/스크립트가 "어느 원본 스킨 소스 파일(CSS/JS)"에서 왔는지 역추적한다. Cafe24 HTML 옵티마이저가 여러 파일을 optimizer.php/optimizer_user.php 번들 하나로 합쳐버려서, 브라우저에서 파일명을 검색해도 원본이 안 보이는 상황을 해결한다. 입력은 CSS 셀렉터 / JS 코드조각·심볼, 또는 "화면의 이 요소" 같은 설명 둘 다 받는다. Use this whenever working on a Cafe24 / SmartDesign skin and you need to know which source file to edit for a style or script you see on the live site — triggers like "이 스타일 어느 파일이야", "이 요소 CSS 원본 찾아줘", "번들에서 원본 파일 찾아", "이 JS 어디서 와", "which file is this rule in", "find the source file for this element", especially when the repo is a partial mirror and the file is… ([sadie100/claude-dotfiles](https://github.com/sadie100/claude-dotfiles/tree/HEAD/skills/cafe24-find-source))
- **cafe24-live-fix**: Use when fixing CSS/JS on a Cafe24 mall with NO FTP integration and no local code mirror — the user gives only a live/test URL plus a requirement (component not showing, style mismatch vs Figma, a selector) and wants the finished code handed back. Triggers - "/cafe24-live-fix", "이 몰은 FTP 연동이 안 돼", "로컬에 코드가 없어", "URL만 줄 테니 고쳐줘", fixing a mall that isn't in any local repo. ([sadie100/claude-dotfiles](https://github.com/sadie100/claude-dotfiles/tree/HEAD/skills/cafe24-live-fix))

MCP cafe24_get 으로 페이지별 응답을 수집하여 CSV(UTF-8 BOM) + xlsx에 누적 append. 최초 실행 시 2026-05-01 ~ 오늘 전체 적재, 이후엔 마지막 적재일+1일 ~ 오늘만 fetch. 사용자가 "주문 적재 계속", "증분 적재", "매일 주문 자동 적재", "orders incremental", "cafe24-orders-incremental"을 호출하거나 "주기적으로 주문 데이터 쌓고 싶어"라고 말하면 동작한다. PII(개인정보) 적재 금지 정책 엄격 준수. ([egnis-it/claude-plugins](https://github.com/egnis-it/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/egnis-cafe24/skills/cafe24-orders-incremental))

- **cai-de-two-column**: 当用户面对一个**具体的人**（继任者、关键岗位候选人、技术合伙人、要提拔的下属），已经掌握一定事实、要在「托付多少」上做决定——德压不压得住才、给多少权限、定谁上——时使用。 把《資治通鑑》周紀一第3段臣光曰史论的才德四分法收成两栏打分：才栏列可验证的能力事实，德栏单列可指名的德性缺口，合判定档（聖人／君子／愚人／小人），再问「若其以才行不仁，谁能制之」，落三选一处置（托付／限用／不用）。 适用：继任选拔、关键岗位任命（碰钱／碰数据／碰核心权限）、合伙人筛选、两个候选人排序、给「能人」定权限。 不做：预测吉凶、算命、给历史人物翻案、道德审判贴标签、下现代合规结论、朗诵道德口号。 不用于：还不了解这个人、要判断「怎么观察他」——那是 zhiren-five-looks（知人五视）的活；或要判断一项已发生举措的连锁反应——那是 tongjian-decision-trace 的活。 English: Use when deciding how much to entrust to a specific person whose facts you already know: score talent and virtue in two separate columns, rank 聖人&gt;君子&gt;愚人&gt;小人, ask who could restrain him if he used… ([yihexiang/rulai-distill](https://github.com/yihexiang/rulai-distill/tree/HEAD/books/tongjian-wei-bundle/skills/cai-de-two-column) · ★2)

- **caijing-fundamental**: 财经·基本面——产出一份分析师级、全面、详细、前瞻、论点驱动的深度研究文档。对 A股/港股上市公司：立核心论点与分歧视角（市场信什么、我哪里不同），在 2–3 个决定命运的胜负手上量化钻透（带估算区间与置信度），十四段全面框架每段有真分析与子项，配三情景前瞻盈利、估值矩阵、多投资哲学对撞、Pre-Mortem 反偏见与长期跟踪清单。默认产出研报级 docx；客户合规版按需。触发："全面分析XX""XX基本面怎么样""这公司质地如何"；命令 /caijing:fundamental。 ([nekopunch11/rodya-caijing-studio](https://github.com/nekopunch11/rodya-caijing-studio/tree/HEAD/caijing-fundamental) · ★47)
- **caijing-industry**: 财经·产业链——只回答"题材链条怎么分、代表公司有哪些"（题材层，不是个股层）。覆盖题材驱动、行业指标、渗透率 S 曲线、上下游图谱、利润池五因子、竞争方式和代表公司纯度；客户合规版保留利润沉淀与供给约束判断，但不做推荐排序。默认产出专业版产业链梳理 docx；客户版图谱卡+文案按需。触发："XX题材梳理""XX产业链""受益标的有哪些""这个概念怎么看"；命令 /caijing:industry。 ([nekopunch11/rodya-caijing-studio](https://github.com/nekopunch11/rodya-caijing-studio/tree/HEAD/caijing-industry) · ★47)
